//============================================================
//  Player leave handoff (P1~P4)
//  - Active players emit a heartbeat every trigger cycle.
//  - The engine reassigns departed Men/Buildings to Neutral(P12); P8 transfers them and the leaver's resources to one survivor.
//  - Survivors with a different starting race are preferred; ties are random.
//============================================================

//----- Heartbeat: triggers owned by a departed player stop refreshing this DC -----//
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Always();
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Current Player", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 1); //접속 생존 신호 : 현재 cycle에 플레이어 트리거가 실행됨
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//----- One-line system notification: code XY means leaver PX -> receiver PY -----//
Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Beacon", Exactly, 12);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>[병력 승계] <08>플레이어 1<04>의 유닛이 -> <0E>플레이어 2<04>에게 이양되었습니다.");
}

Trigger("Player 1", "Player 2", "Player 3", "Player 4"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Beacon", Exactly, 13);
Actions:
	Display Text Message(Always Display, "<07>[병력 승계] <08>플레이어 1<04>의 유닛이 -> <0F>플레이어 3<04>에게 이양되었습니다.");
}

//----- Register only slots that have actually emitted a heartbeat -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch162", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 1", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch162", set); //P1 참가 확인 : 처음부터 빈 슬롯은 이탈 감시에서 제외
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch163", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch163", set); //P2 참가 확인 : 처음부터 빈 슬롯은 이탈 감시에서 제외
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch164", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 3", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch164", set); //P3 참가 확인 : 처음부터 빈 슬롯은 이탈 감시에서 제외
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Switch("Switch165", Not Set);
	Deaths("Player 4", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch165", set); //P4 참가 확인 : 처음부터 빈 슬롯은 이탈 감시에서 제외
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//플레이어 메시지 출력이 끝난 뒤 다음 cycle용 알림 코드 초기화
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Beacon", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//원 소유자의 heartbeat가 다시 보이면 오판으로 간주하고 승계 상태 전체 취소
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 1", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch166", clear); //P1 생존 확인 : 이탈 처리 완료 표식 복구
	Set Deaths("Player 1", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 2", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 3", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 4", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 0); //P1 승계 작업 취소 : 유닛 Give 차단
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 2);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch167", clear); //P2 생존 확인 : 이탈 처리 완료 표식 복구
	Set Deaths("Player 1", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 2", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 3", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 4", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 0); //P2 승계 작업 취소 : 유닛 Give 차단
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 3);
	Deaths("Player 3", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch168", clear); //P3 생존 확인 : 이탈 처리 완료 표식 복구
	Set Deaths("Player 1", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 2", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);

//----- Exact resource handoff: P8 transfers resources before ownership finalization -----//
// P8 owns every resource action so empty/faked recipient slots do not skip the transfer.

// P1 ore -> P2
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Protoss Beacon", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 1", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
	Accumulate("Player 1", At least, 524288, ore);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 1", Subtract, 524288, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 524288, ore);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Protoss Beacon", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 1", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
	Accumulate("Player 1", At least, 262144, ore);
Actions:
	Set Resources("Player 1", Subtract, 262144, ore);
	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 262144, ore);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 1);

// P1 gas -> P2

Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Protoss Beacon", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 1", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);

	Set Resources("Player 2", Add, 524288, gas);
	Preserve Trigger();

//----- Finalize only while recipient is alive and original owner heartbeat is still absent -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Protoss Beacon", Exactly, 1);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Zerg Beacon", At least, 1);
	Deaths("Player 1", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
Actions:
	Give Units to Player("Player 12", "Player 2", "Zerg Larva", All, "Anywhere"); //Larva is outside Men; transfer it explicitly before its Hatchery/Lair/Hive
	Give Units to Player("Player 12", "Player 2", "Zerg Egg", All, "Anywhere"); //transfer Zerg morphs that are outside Men
	Give Units to Player("Player 12", "Player 2", "Cocoon", All, "Anywhere");
	Give Units to Player("Player 12", "Player 2", "Zerg Lurker Egg", All, "Anywhere");
	Give Units to Player("Player 12", "Player 2", "Buildings", All, "Anywhere"); //departed assets are Neutral-owned; transfer all buildings to the selected recipient
	Give Units to Player("Player 12", "Player 2", "Men", All, "Anywhere"); //departed assets are Neutral-owned; transfer all men to the same selected recipient
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Beacon", Set To, 12);
	Set Deaths("Player 2", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Terran Beacon", Set To, 31);
	Set Deaths("Player 1", "Protoss Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

//----- Queue one newly departed player at a time after two consecutive misses (P1 -> P4 priority) -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 5);
	Switch("Switch162", Set);
	Switch("Switch166", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch173", Set);
	Deaths("Player 1", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch166", set); //P1 이탈 1회 등록 : 같은 자산을 중복 이양하지 않음
	Set Switch("Switch173", clear); //P1 2회 연속 heartbeat 누락 확인 완료
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 1);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 5);
	Switch("Switch163", Set);
	Switch("Switch167", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch174", Set);
	Deaths("Player 2", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch167", set); //P2 이탈 1회 등록 : 같은 자산을 중복 이양하지 않음
	Set Switch("Switch174", clear); //P2 2회 연속 heartbeat 누락 확인 완료
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 2);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
	Elapsed Time(At least, 5);
	Switch("Switch164", Set);
	Switch("Switch168", Not Set);
	Switch("Switch175", Set);
	Deaths("Player 3", "Zerg Beacon", Exactly, 0);
Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch168", set); //P3 이탈 1회 등록 : 같은 자산을 중복 이양하지 않음
	Set Switch("Switch175", clear); //P3 2회 연속 heartbeat 누락 확인 완료
	Set Deaths("Player 8", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 3);
	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Switch("Switch162", Set);

	Switch("Switch173", Not Set);

Actions:
	Set Switch("Switch173", set); //P1 heartbeat 1차 누락 기록 : 아직 자산 이양 금지
	Preserve Trigger();
}

	Set Switch("Switch171", randomize);

}

//----- P8 closes the cycle after every leave/selection check has read heartbeat=1 -----//
Trigger("Player 8"){
Conditions:
	Always();
Actions:
	Set Deaths("Player 1", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 2", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 3", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Set Deaths("Player 4", "Zerg Beacon", Set To, 0);
	Preserve Trigger();
}
